### 2026-09-30T05:04:25Z [**wxxsfxyzm/InstallerX-Revived**](https://github.com/wxxsfxyzm/InstallerX-Revived)

```
[English](#english) | [中文](#chinese)  <a id="english"></a>  <!-- English changelog begin -->  ## Highlights  * One APK for online and offline use, with configurable network access and download modes * More control over signature analysis, profile authorization, and installation sources * Operation history, backup and restore, and installed-module version comparison * Better installation compatibility, archived-app handling in system package manager mode, and stability fixes * 26.09 is the last release supporting Android 8.0 and 8.1  ---  ## What's Changed  ### New Features  * Added: network source modes: Full download (default), Smart, and Low storage. A single APK can be streamed when supported; package archives are still downloaded in full. Streaming cannot perform full APK signature verification; Android performs the final check. Choose Full download when full pre-install signature analysis is needed. @Shikochin * Added: configurable app signature analysis, a separate split-package analysis toggle, and details such as certificate summaries, signature schemes, and verification issues. App signature analysis is on by default; split-package analysis is off. On supported system SDKs, InstallerX can detect Android 17 v3.2 and ML-DSA signatures. * Added: Oplus OS SDK compatibility checks on OPPO and OnePlus devices when **Show OEM extra info** is enabled. If the device version is too low, InstallerX shows both versions and disables installation. * Added: an Unknown scope to handle abnormal installation behavior on certain OPPO and vivo systems. * Added: Smart authorization, which tries enabled fallback authorizers in the order chosen by the user. None is available only when system session installation is supported. * Added: Auto-approve session installs. It is disabled by default and should only be enabled for trusted sources. Install requester settings can also use no override, the initiating app, or a selected installed app. * Added: install pre-approval support and improved existing permission-confirmation flows. Apps that request an installation result now receive success, failure, or cancellation results, improving compatibility with third-party callers. * Added: operation history. You can enable or disable it, keep or clear existing records when disabling it, and control history indicators separately. Up to 100 records are retained. * Added: backup and restore for profiles, scopes, app settings, and history. Backups are validated before a confirmed restore replaces the current settings. * Added: detection of installed Magisk, KernelSU, and APatch modules, with version comparison while installing a new module. * Added: archived-app restoration and removal of archived entries when running as the system package manager on Android 15 and later. Low storage, offline operation, and an unavailable original installer now have actionable messages. * Added: install-dialog display options to show only differences or always show old and new values, and to expand temporary settings by default.  ### Improvements  * Improved: install-source identification and unknown-source permission prompts, including clearer messages when the requesting app is hidden. Fixed user-list loading with custom authorizers and restricted install options that Dhizuku does not support. * Improved: local APK, APKS, APKM, XAPK, and ZIP parsing to reduce unnecessary temporary files and repeated extraction, with better handling of unusual ZIP formats. Improved split selection and batch analysis, and added a separate writing-progress stage during installation. * Improved: queuing for consecutive install, uninstall, and session-confirmation requests, strengthened confirmation-source checks, and prevented repeated submissions. Fixed stale notifications during rapid stage changes and background actions triggered while a session is closing. * Improved: Material 3 and Miuix settings, back, and sheet interactions. Settings better adapt to font scaling, and the page no longer scrolls while reordering items. The floating bottom bar now supports keyboard navigation and accessibility.  ### Changes  * Changed: online and offline packages are unified into one APK. Use **Allow Internet access** in Network settings to control update checks and network downloads. Release filenames still include `online` for compatibility with in-app updates. * Changed: existing signature policies are now named **Block Signature Mismatch** and **Block Unknown Signature**. Both blocks are disabled by default for new profiles. * Changed: 26.09 is the last version supporting Android 8.0 and 8.1. Future versions will require Android 9 or later. * Changed: automatic default installer locking was removed following a fix to the locking implementation. * Changed: **Hide launcher icon** moved to Basic settings. Home now shows improved device names and includes a **Learn More** entry.  ### Fixes  * Fixed: missing scopes when undoing a profile deletion; restoring the profile also restores its source-app associations. * Fixed: split and dex metadata selections are restored when an app is deselected and reselected during batch installation. Reopening results from a notification also restores the selected app’s details. * Fixed: concurrent ZIP entry reads and stale system file index entries after deleting install packages. * Fixed: Android 17+ self-updates now recover their state after the process is killed, show the final result, and clean up source files. * Fixed: biometric authentication on Android 8.x @Ephemera42 * Fixed: a possible crash when install or uninstall screens request portrait orientation on Android 8.0. * Fixed: the scale animation when navigating back and the status shown when reinstalling the same app version @AlexLiuDev233 * Fixed: duplicated bottom padding on the Settings page @youfeng11 * Fixed: default installer locking with Dhizuku. * Fixed: transparent Miuix bar backgrounds on Android 12 and earlier @AlexLiuDev233, and dynamic-color fallback on Android 11 and earlier @budingxiaocai.  ### Docs & Translations  * Translations: updated text across multiple languages.  <!-- English changelog end -->  ---  <a id="chinese"></a>  <!-- 中文更新日志开始 -->  ## 亮点  * 联网版与离线版合并为一个 APK，可自行控制联网和下载方式 * 更灵活的签名分析、配置授权与安装来源设置 * 新增操作记录、备份还原，以及已安装模块的版本比较 * 改善安装兼容性、系统包管理模式下的归档应用处理和运行稳定性 * 26.09 是最后一个支持 Android 8.0/8.1 的版本  ---  ## 更新内容  ### 新增功能  * 新增：网络源处理模式，包括完整下载（默认）、智能和低存储占用。支持条件满足时，单个 APK 可以流式处理，应用包归档仍会完整下载。流式处理无法执行完整 APK 验签，最终由系统校验；需要完整的安装前签名分析时请选择“完整下载” @Shikochin * 新增：可配置的应用签名分析、独立的拆分包签名分析开关，以及证书摘要、签名方案和验证问题等详情。应用签名分析默认开启，拆分包分析默认关闭。在受支持的系统 SDK 上，可以检测 Android 17 v3.2 和 ML-DSA 签名。 * 新增：在 OPPO 和 OnePlus 设备上，启用“展示 OEM 附加信息”后，会在安装前检查 APK 要求的 Oplus OS SDK 版本。设备版本较低时显示版本差异并禁止安装。 * 新增：支持未知来源作用域，用于处理部分 OPPO/vivo 系统的异常安装行为。 * 新增：智能授权，可按用户设定的顺序尝试后备授权器；“无特权”仅在支持系统会话安装时可用。 * 新增：支持自动批准会话安装，默认关闭，仅应对可信来源启用。安装请求方也可以选择不设置、沿用发起方或指定设备上已安装的应用。 * 新增：安装预批准支持，并完善已有的权限确认流程。向请求安装结果的应用返回成功、失败或取消结果，改善第三方应用调用安装器的兼容性。 * 新增：操作记录，可自由开启或关闭；关闭时可以保留或清除已有记录，也可以单独控制记录指示器，最多保留 100 条记录。 * 新增：支持导出和还原配置文件、作用域、应用设置及历史记录。还原前会校验备份，并在确认后替换当前设置。 * 新增：安装新模块时，可以检测已安装的 Magisk、KernelSU 和 APatch 模块并比较版本。 * 新增：在 Android 15 及以上，作为系统包管理运行时，补齐对归档应用的处理能力，支持恢复归档应用和删除归档条目；存储不足、离线、原安装器不可用等情况会显示对应的处理提示。 * 新增：安装对话框显示选项：可以只显示差异或始终显示新旧值，也可以默认展开临时设置面板。  ### 优化与改进  * 优化：安装来源识别和未知来源授权提示。发起安装的应用被隐藏时，会给出更明确的提示；修复自定义授权器下的用户列表读取，并限制 Dhizuku 不支持的安装选项。 * 优化：本地 APK、APKS、APKM、XAPK 和 ZIP 解析，减少不必要的临时文件和重复解压，并改善部分特殊 ZIP 格式的兼容性。改进分包选择和批量分析，安装时单独展示写入进度。 * 优化：连续安装、卸载及会话确认请求的排队处理，加强确认来源校验，避免重复提交；修复安装阶段快速切换时通知残留，以及会话关闭时仍触发后台操作的问题。 * 优化：Material 3 和 Miuix 的设置展开、返回和弹层交互；改进设置项的字体缩放适配，并避免拖动排序时页面同时滚动。浮动底栏新增键盘操作和无障碍支持。  ### 变更  * 变更：联网版和离线版合并为一个 APK。通过网络设置中的联网开关控制更新检查和网络下载安装；发布文件名仍保留 `online`，以兼容应用内更新。 * 变更：配置中的签名策略开关改为“禁止安装签名不匹配的应用”和“禁止安装签名未知的应用”，新配置默认关闭这两项拦截。 * 变更：26.09 是最后一个支持 Android 8.0/8.1 的版本，后续版本最低要求 Android 9。 * 变更：修复安装器锁定实现后，移除自动锁定功能。 * 变更：“隐藏桌面图标”移至基础设置；主页改进设备名称显示，并增加“了解更多”入口。  ### 修复  * 修复：撤销删除配置后作用域丢失的问题，恢复配置时也会恢复原有的来源应用关联。 * 修复：批量安装对话框中，取消后重新选中同一应用，可恢复此次分析中此前的分包和 dex 元数据选择；从通知重新打开安装结果时，也会恢复选中应用的详情。 * 修复：ZIP 并发读取问题；删除安装包后同步清理系统文件索引，减少文件已删除却仍显示在列表中的情况。 * 修复：Android 17+ 自更新时，即使进程被系统终止，也可以恢复更新状态、显示结果并清理源文件。 * 修复：Android 8.x 生物识别认证问题 @Ephemera42 * 修复：Android 8.0 安装/卸载界面切换竖屏时可能闪退的问题。 * 修复：页面返回时的缩放动画，以及重新安装同版本应用时误显示为“升级”的问题 @AlexLiuDev233 * 修复：设置页底部重复边距 @youfeng11 * 修复：Dhizuku 下的默认安装器锁定问题。 * 修复：Android 12 及以下的 Miuix 栏背景透明问题 @AlexLiuDev233，以及 Android 11 及以下动态取色回退异常 @budingxiaocai。  ### 文档与翻译  * 翻译：更新多语言翻译。  <!-- 中文更新日志结束 --> 
```

The command remains supported for now.  ## What's Changed  - chore: bump version to 1.0.13 - community: add Raaghu Spec Kit Azure DevOps extension to friends (#4782) - feat(extensions): add bundled `github` extension for taskstoissues (#4488) - fix(integrations): drop invalid --model/--output-format flags from Vibe dispatch (#4784) - Update Superpowers Implementation Bridge extension to v1.3.0 (#4783) - fix(workflows): evaluate parenthesised expressions (#4417) - fix(workflows): resolve negative list indices in expressions (#4416) - docs: explain how Spec Kit uses an agentic SDLC (#4774) - fix(copilot): detect copilot.exe on Windows instead of assuming copilot.cmd (#4758) - fix: keep non-ASCII text readable in merged JSON settings files (#4773) - Added openspec extension (#4765) - feat(integrations): add MiniMax Code (mcode) integration (#4644) (#4645) - docs(community): add Specstride to Community Friends (#4762) - docs: clarify feature directories may live outside the project root (#4739) - fix(scripts): honor SPECKIT_PYTHON override for preset manifest parsing (#4445) - chore: clarify stale issue and PR guidance (#4772) - Update OWASP LLM Threat Model extension to v2.1.2 (#4771) - docs: correct workflow publishing security-review claim, add catalog vetting notes (#4736) - [preset] Update Intake Sequencing Governance preset to v0.2.6 (#4761) - [extension] Add Test Validation extension to community catalog (#4760) - chore: release 1.0.12, begin 1.0.13.dev0 development (#4759)

### 2026-09-27T21:51:04Z [**Huanshere/VideoLingo**](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo)

```
# VideoLingo v3.1.1 ✨  More flexible subtitle workflows, safer long-video dubbing, and official Fish Audio voice cloning.  ### 🚀 What’s new  - **Official Fish Audio dubbing is here.** The new `fish_audio` provider uses Fish Audio’s official API and defaults to the `s2.1-pro` model. It can clone the voice from 15–30 seconds of the source video without storing a voice in your Fish Audio account, or use a fixed Fish Audio voice or custom voice ID. - **OpenAI-compatible TTS can now use OpenLux.** The OpenAI TTS backend supports the OpenLux speech endpoint, with configurable base URL and model. - **Bring your own subtitles.** Import an SRT file for translation, with or without a video, and use transcribe-only mode when you only need source subtitles. - **Review the pipeline before it continues.** Optional checkpoints let you edit terminology before translation and review the translation spreadsheet before subtitle generation. - **More control over subtitle output.** Set a translation style, customize font/size/color, and independently choose bilingual, translated, or source subtitles for the subtitle and dubbed videos. - **MAI-Transcribe-2 is available as an optional ASR backend** through Azure Speech or OpenRouter, while local Qwen3-ASR remains the default. - **Long videos are more robust.** Demucs processes long audio in chunks, Japanese NLP is batched, subtitle splitting has safer fallbacks, and translation/punctuation edge cases no longer stop the pipeline. - **Dubbing failures are visible and recoverable.** Failed TTS requests stop after retries instead of becoming silent lines. Speaking speed is capped, lines that still cannot fit are listed in `output/log/dub_truncated.json`, and dubbed videos are produced correctly even when burned subtitles are disabled.  ### ⚠️ Before upgrading  - The old `fish_tts` provider for 302.ai and `azure_tts` have been removed. Choose Fish Audio, Edge TTS, or another available provider; Edge TTS is now the default. - Fish Audio requires `fish_audio.api_key` and an account with credit. `msgpack` is a new dependency and is installed with the normal setup flow. - The Qwen3-ASR model size is now an advanced `config.yaml` option: `whisper.qwen_model`.  ### ✅ Release validation  - Automated suite: **663 passed, 19 skipped, 12 subtests passed**. - Five real English videos (about 7.5–11.3 minutes each) were processed end to end into Simplified Chinese, Japanese, French, Spanish, and Traditional Chinese using OpenLux GPT-5.6 Luna and Fish Audio `s2.1-pro` voice cloning. - All **1,025** translated lines produced TTS audio, with **0 truncated lines**. All five dubbed videos passed full video/audio decode checks and frame inspection.  ---  # VideoLingo v3.1.1 ✨  字幕流程更灵活，长视频配音更稳定，并正式接入 Fish Audio 声音克隆。  ### 🚀 这次更新  - **新增 Fish Audio 官方配音。** 新的 `fish_audio` Provider 直接调用 Fish Audio 官方 API，默认模型为 `s2.1-pro`。可以从原视频中取 15–30 秒语音克隆音色，不会在 Fish Audio 账户中保存声音；也可以选择固定音色或自定义 Voice ID。 - **OpenAI 兼容 TTS 现在可以使用 OpenLux。** OpenAI TTS 后端支持 OpenLux 的语音端点，并可自定义 Base URL 和模型。 - **直接使用现有字幕。** 可导入 SRT 进行翻译，无论是否有视频；也可只跑转录阶段，仅生成原文字幕。 - **流程可以暂停检查。** 可选择在翻译前修改术语表，也可在生成字幕前检查并修改翻译表格。 - **字幕输出更可控。** 新增翻译风格，可调整字体、字号和颜色，并可分别为字幕视频和配音视频选择双语、译文或原文字幕。 - **新增可选的 MAI-Transcribe-2 识别后端**，支持 Azure Speech 和 OpenRouter；默认仍然是本地 Qwen3-ASR。 - **长视频处理更稳定。** Demucs 会分块处理长音频，日语 NLP 支持分批，字幕切分有更稳妥的降级方案，翻译和标点边界情况不再让整条流程中断。 - **配音失败不再被隐藏。** TTS 重试失败后会明确中止，不会生成静音冒充成功。语速有上限，仍然放不下的句子会写入 `output/log/dub_truncated.json`；关闭烧录字幕时也能正常生成配音视频。  ### ⚠️ 升级前留意  - 302.ai 的旧 `fish_tts` 和 `azure_tts` 已移除。请改用 Fish Audio、Edge TTS 或其他可用 Provider；默认已改为 Edge TTS。 - Fish Audio 需要配置 `fish_audio.api_key`，账户也需要有可用额度。新依赖 `msgpack` 会随正常安装流程自动安装。 - Qwen3-ASR 模型大小现在是 `config.yaml` 中的高级选项：`whisper.qwen_model`。  ### ✅ 发布验证  - 自动化测试：**663 passed，19 skipped，12 subtests passed**。 - 使用 5 个真实英文视频（每个约 7.5–11.3 分钟）完整跑通简体中文、日语、法语、西班牙语和繁体中文，翻译使用 OpenLux GPT-5.6 Luna，配音使用 Fish Audio `s2.1-pro` 声音克隆。 - 全部 **1,025** 条译文都成功生成 TTS，**0 条被截断**。5 个配音成片全部通过完整音视频解码和抽帧检查。  Full Changelog / 完整改动：[v3.1.0...v3.1.1](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/compare/v3.1.0...v3.1.1) 
```

```
# 更新内容  新增功能与改进  1. 增强信令重连后的会话恢复能力，音频和数据通道新增协商式 SRTP 加密支持。 2. 新增被控端版本检查，连接旧版远程主机时提示升级。 3. 桌面端新增“保存远程画面预览”开关，关闭后清除已有预览，保留连接记录和密码；桌面端和 iOS 支持在无预览时显示被控端平台信息。 4. 优化连接状态标记、最近连接卡片、更新提示图标及公告窗口样式。 5. 桌面端和 iOS 新增公告收件箱，支持未读提示、已读记录、删除公告及打开相关链接。 6. Linux 新增 Wayland 会话授权说明及切换 X11 的使用指引。 7. iOS 新增会话内画面设置，支持实时调整画质、采集帧率和画面偏好，无需断开重连。 8. iOS H.264 编解码统一使用 VideoToolbox。 9. 完善 iOS 网络状态展示，支持查看连接方式、收发码率、丢包率、帧率、连接延迟、视频延迟及媒体加密状态。 10. 完善 iOS 隐私同意流程、远程预览保存控制及第三方许可证与源码信息。  问题修复  1. 修复 Linux X11 下远程窗口拖动异常。 2. 修复 macOS 屏幕采集回调可能发生的死锁，改善 macOS 14 的兼容性。 3. 修复 macOS 主窗口标题栏显示异常。 4. 修复 iOS 设置页面导航问题，完善服务器地址和端口校验。
```

### 2026-09-23T21:44:30Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.13.2  This release makes `/opsx:verify` report what it actually checked, makes archive reliable on Windows, and fixes workflow guidance across several tools.  ### Improved  - **Task guidance** - Each task group now includes its own tests and documentation updates instead of deferring them to a final group, and onboarding teaches the same rule. - **Approval prompts** - Fast-forward asks for clarification only when context is critically unclear, and onboarding asks you to approve the task breakdown before saving it. - **Scenario-loss messages** - When `openspec validate` or `openspec archive` flags a `MODIFIED` block that drops scenarios, it now also says what the block adds. - **Codex setup hint** - The setup hint is clearer for Codex CLI, IDE, and desktop app users.  ### Fixed  - **Verify** - `/opsx:verify` no longer reports skipped checks as passing, and no longer tells agents to re-implement a requirement the change removed. Removed requirements pass once the behavior is gone, and renamed requirements are checked against their original behavior. - **Archive on Windows** - Archive no longer leaves `.openspec-archive.lock` behind, and it finishes instead of rolling back when Windows blocks renaming a change directory. - **Line endings** - Rewriting a CRLF spec, `.bashrc`, `.zshrc`, or `CLAUDE.md` keeps its existing line endings, so Windows users no longer get whole-file diffs. - **Archive task warnings** - Agent-driven archive uses schema-aware task progress, so custom task files and globs still trigger incomplete-task warnings. - **Artifact outputs** - Artifact output patterns with brace expansion or extglob now resolve, while literal filenames still work. - **`/opsx:update`** - Update can now fill a missing file under a glob artifact that is already partly written, instead of pointing you to a step that could not do it. - **Workflow schema labels** - Workflows no longer display a schema name that `openspec list --json` does not return. - **Kilo Code** - Commands are generated in `.kilo/command/`, where Kilo Code reads them. `openspec init` and legacy cleanup remove the files OpenSpec generated in `.kilocode/workflows/`, matched by their known file names (including copies you edited), and leave other files in place. - **Continue** - Continue commands no longer lead local models to call a tool named after the active workflow. - **Validation** - `validate --strict` no longer treats a Spanish or Portuguese Purpose starting with "Todo" as a placeholder. - **Codex update** - `openspec update --force` now exits non-zero when it cannot replace a legacy-only Codex install.  ## New Contributors  * @xiazl1993 made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1146 * @kikeprzn made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1769 * @VeryComplexAndLongName made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1934 * @philo-x made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1885 * @guillaume-flambard made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1845 * @Tyagiquamar made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1912 * @runsonmypc made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1733 * @kevin9327 made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1926  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.13.1...v1.13.2 
```

### 2026-09-29T09:30:20Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.8.1 is a security release. It makes `tuios ssh` and `tuios-web` safer to expose, and it limits what a pane and its output can do outside the pane. It also brings hints mode, multi copy mode and the fixes merged since v0.8.0.  Before you upgrade, read [Breaking changes and migration](#breaking-changes-and-migration). Some commands and configs that worked in v0.8.0 are refused now.  The full list is at [tuios.dev/releases](https://tuios.dev/releases).  ## Install  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.8.1 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.8.1   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`.  ## Security fixes  ### Remote access  - **`tuios ssh` keys.** `tuios ssh` does not accept a key that has options, such as `command=`, `from=` or `restrict`. tuios cannot apply these options, so it refuses the key. The log names the line of each refused key. - **`tuios ssh` keys file.** `tuios ssh` reads only `~/.config/tuios/authorized_keys`. It does not read `~/.ssh/authorized_keys` unless you name that file with `--authorized-keys`. - **`tuios-web` passwords.** `tuios-web` can ask for a password. Use `--random-password`, `--password-file` or the `TUIOS_WEB_PASSWORD` environment variable. `--user` sets the user name. The default user name is `tuios`. - **Host header.** On `localhost`, `tuios-web` accepts a session only when the Host header names this machine. Use `--allow-host` to add the name of a reverse proxy. - **Empty `--host`.** An empty `--host` listens on every interface. `tuios ssh` and `tuios-web` now apply the rules for a network address to it. - **pprof.** `--pprof :6060` listens on 127.0.0.1 only. To listen on every interface, give `0.0.0.0:6060`. - **Worktree patches.** `tuios worktree pull` keeps a patch that did not apply in a new file with a random name. Only you can read the file. The message shows its path.  ### Pane grants  - **More callers check the grants.** Pane grants now cover the link sockets, the tmux shim's pane holder, prompts, `send-keys`, `run-command` and typing through the client protocol. - **Prompts.** A pane without the `respond` grant cannot type into another pane that waits on a prompt. This applies to a pane with `admin` too. - **`send-keys` with `PREFIX`.** `send-keys` with `PREFIX` from a pane is refused. The message names the commands to use instead. - **Inbox answers.** The Inbox refuses answers that `send-keys` typed. This covers the digit keys, the second press of a risky allow, question answers and the release of held mail. - **Link sockets.** A process inside a pane, or a process that the daemon started, cannot connect to a link socket. - **Wider grants wait.** A `config.toml` change that widens `[agents.permissions]`, `[hosts]` or a link policy waits for `tuios config apply`. Run it from a terminal outside tuios. A daemon restart also applies it.   - A change that narrows these tables applies at once.   - The Inbox shows an item when a change waits. `tuios pane-grants` and the log say so too.   - A daemon start that finds wider grants than the last run says so in the log, in `tuios pane-grants` and in the Inbox.   - A host that you add with "Add a host" on the settings page applies at once. - **`ssh_options`.** A host's `ssh_options` accepts only safe options, each with one plain value, written as `-o Keyword=value`.   - tuios drops a host with a refused option. The Inbox and `tuios hosts` name the reason.   - `ProxyCommand`, `LocalCommand`, the known hosts files, `ControlPath` and `-F` are refused.   - Port forwards (`-L`, `-R`, `-D`) are refused.   - `-J` and `ProxyJump` take host names only. `ForwardAgent` takes only `yes` or `no`.   - A host `addr` or `command` cannot start with a dash.  ### Pane output  - **Clipboard writes.** The new option `appearance.selection.osc52_write` controls what OSC 52 from a pane does. The values are `off`, `ask`, `focused` and `on`. The default is `focused`.   - With `focused`, the focused pane sets the host clipboard. A write from another pane shows a message in the dock. Click the message to copy the text.   - Each pane keeps one clipboard ask. A click copies only the text that the dock shows. - **Control characters.** Pastes, notifications, OSC 66 text and link addresses lose their control characters. Tabs and line breaks in a paste stay. - **Clipboard replies.** tuios drops a clipboard reply that answers no read request of tuios. - **Kitty graphics files.** A kitty graphics file must belong to you, and its path must be absolute. tuios does not read from `/proc`, `/sys` or `/dev`, other than `/dev/shm`. A `t=t` file name must hold `tty-graphics-protocol`. - **"cd here" and layout load.** These type a `cd` only into a pane whose shell is at its prompt. In daemon mode, the daemon does this check.   - tuios types no `cd` for a folder whose name holds a quote, a backslash or a control character. The dock says when it did not type.   - Layout load starts each new window in its folder. It does not type a `cd` into a new window.  ## New features  - **Hints mode.** The leader and `F` label the copyable text on the focused pane, such as URLs, paths, hashes and IP addresses. Type a label to copy its text.   - Shift and a label also types the text into the pane. Ctrl and a label opens a URL or a local path.   - The `[hints]` table sets the patterns, the label alphabet, the open command and the dim. - **Multi copy mode.** The copy mode key enters multi copy mode when the focused pane is in a multifocus set of two or more panes.   - Each pane gets its own cursor and selection. A search moves each pane to its own match.   - `y` copies the selection of each pane. `Tab` cycles the format. `Y` writes the text to a new file.   - `appearance.selection.multi_format` sets the format: `plain`, `markdown` or `json`. - **Multifocus.**   - A paste goes to every pane in the multifocus set.   - The new actions `toggle_multifocus_active` and `toggle_multifocus_all` put panes in the set or take them out. They have no default key.   - With `appearance.dim_unfocused` on, panes in the set are not dimmed. Set `appearance.dim_multifocus` to dim them again. - **Copy mode.**   - Copy mode starts on the terminal cursor. Set `appearance.selection.copy_entry = "center"` to start on the middle row.   - `/` searches forward and `?` searches back. `n` repeats the search in its direction, and `N` goes the other way.   - The new actions `copy_mode_search_forward` and `copy_mode_search_backward` enter copy mode and open the search. They have no default key. - **Focus with `j` and `k`.** In window mode, `j` and `k` move focus down and up. A config that already binds `j` or `k` there keeps its binding. - **Agent mail.**   - The mailbox shows each message body inside an untrusted content fence. Each thread shows its id.   - `n` in the mailbox writes a new message to an agent in the session.   - The new `[notifications.mail]` table controls mail alerts. - **Other machines.**   - Results from another machine are marked as untrusted. `capture-pane` takes `--json`.   - `send-agent-message --attach` to a host puts each file in the far session's stash. - **Agents.** tuios reports the state of oh-my-pi. The detection commands report the age of their evidence. - **Keyboard layouts.** Bindings match the physical key under any keyboard layout, non-Latin layouts too.  ## Fixes  - **Nested attach.** tuios refuses to attach a session from inside its own pane, or from a pane of a session that shows it. `tuios attach --force` skips the check.   - A terminal window that you start from a pane can attach.   - New panes do not get the variables of an outer tuios pane. - **Layout sync.** Clients send each change to the BSP tree to the daemon, which applies and numbers it ([#230](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/230)). Two clients that change one layout at the same time now end on the same layout. A v0.8.0 client in the same session still works. - **Focus.** The neighbour search steps to a pane that shares only one row with the focused pane. It does not step to a diagonal pane. - **Global sessions.**   - The session switcher switches between sessions on other machines.   - A machine pick goes to the daemon that can act on it.   - One link carries more than fifteen remote panes. - **Nix.** The Nix package builds again with the correct vendor hash. CI checks the hash on every dependency change. - **Keys.**   - Keypad keys, Begin and F13 and above reach the pane.   - The leader pressed twice sends the configured leader to the pane.   - Modifier aliases work in the leader key and in every key entry. - **Web client.** The web client gets the last output of a pane. - **Also fixed.** The copy sweep runs at the frame rate. The session rail fits narrow rows. Focus survives a stale push from another client.  ## Breaking changes and migration  ### tuios ssh  - `tuios ssh` does not start when there is no keys file. This applies to `localhost` too. Before, a server on `localhost` with no keys file let every connection in. - If your key is only in `~/.ssh/authorized_keys`, `tuios ssh` does not use it now. Do one of these:   - Add your public key to `~/.config/tuios/authorized_keys`:      bash     mkdir -p ~/.config/tuios     cat ~/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub >> ~/.config/tuios/authorized_keys           Use your public key file if it has a different name.   - Start with `--authorized-keys ~/.ssh/authorized_keys`. tuios does not apply the rules in `sshd_config` to these keys.   - Start with `--no-auth`. Then every user on this machine can connect, or everyone who reaches the port on a network address. - A key with options in the keys file is not accepted. Add the key again with no options if it must open a tuios session.  ### tuios-web  - A bind to an address other than `localhost` needs a password. `--auto-tls` or `--cert` alone is not enough, because TLS does not check who connects. Add `--random-password` or `--password-file`, or add `--no-auth` on a network you trust. - `--allow-host` needs a password or `--no-auth`. It works only with a loopback `--host`. Give the host name with no port. - On `localhost` with no password, `tuios-web` still starts. It prints one line to say that other users on this machine can connect. - The password file must belong to you, with mode 600 or 400.  ### Pane grants  - **Prompts.** A pane that types into another pane's prompt needs the `respond` grant. `admin` does not include it. To give it to one pane, use `tuios set-pane-grants` or `--grants`. - **`send-keys` with `PREFIX`.** A script in a pane that sends `PREFIX` fails now. Use the tuios command for the action instead, such as `focus-window`, `new-window` or `split-window`. - **`send-keys` with no window.** From a pane, `send-keys` with no window types into the focused pane. tuios does not read these keys as key bindings. - **`run-command`.** From a pane without `respond`, `run-command` cannot type or press keys. Use `send-keys` or `send-text` with `-w`. - **Config changes that widen.** A change that widens `[agents.permissions]`, `[hosts]` or a link policy waits. Run this from a terminal outside tuios:    bash   tuios config apply       `tuios hosts add` from a pane also waits for it.  ### ssh_options  - Look in the Inbox or run `tuios hosts` after the upgrade. A host with a refused option is dropped. - Move a refused option, such as `ProxyCommand` or `LocalForward`, into `~/.ssh/config`. Then name that host in `addr`. - Keep port forwards (`-L`, `-R`, `-D`) in `~/.ssh/config`. - Write each option as `-o Keyword=value`, with one value and no quotes.  ### Clipboard and folders  - **OSC 52.** A program in a background pane cannot set the host clipboard without a click. To get the v0.8.0 behaviour, set `appearance.selection.osc52_write = "on"`. - **"cd here" and layout load.** tuios types no `cd` into a pane that runs a program. Go back to the shell prompt and try again. - **Mixed versions.** A v0.8.1 client with a v0.8.0 daemon types no `cd` into a daemon pane. To start a v0.8.1 daemon, run `tuios kill-server` after the upgrade. This closes every pane in every session, so save your work first.  ## Contributors  Thank you to everyone who sent a pull request for this release:  - @masshirodev: keypad keys, Begin and F13 and above ([#195](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/195)) - @nathan-poncet: chords read by the key they type ([#209](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/209)) - @fonnesbeck: oh-my-pi agent state ([#223](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/223))  Thanks to JakeChop for reporting the security issues.  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.8.0...v0.8.1 
```

### 2026-09-28T03:15:23Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.7.0  GPUI Kit v0.7.0 adds Toolbar, Questionnaire and TimeField, and makes window startup and overlay hosting consistent through a Base-owned Root. Plot primitives are now available without the styled component layer, while Input, TextView and charts gain richer editing, search, date/time and data-visualization APIs. This release also improves Dock interactions, accessibility, streaming and large-data performance; review the Breaking Changes below before upgrading.  ## New components  - **Toolbar and ToolbarGroup**: compose application command rows with source-order controls, semantic groups, separators and flexible spacers. Left/Right moves focus through enabled controls with wrapping. The styled toolbar propagates its density to hosted controls and gives Buttons compact ghost presentation; `child`/`children` accept sized controls, while `content`/`contents` accept labels, separators and custom content. The default size is Small; XSmall and Medium are supported, and Large uses Medium. Disabling toolbar navigation does not disable its controls automatically. ([#3128](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3128))  - **Questionnaire**: build single-choice, multiple-choice and freeform questionnaires around `QuestionnaireState`, with composable question, choice, input, progress and action parts. The component handles validation, optional skipping, keyboard shortcuts, focus, navigation and submission, using existing Radio, Checkbox, Input and Button controls. ([#2878](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2878))  - **TimeField and date/time picking**: edit segmented hours and minutes, optionally seconds, using `TimeFieldState`, `TimePrecision::{Minute, Second}` and `HourCycle::{H23, H12}`. A styled TimeField works independently in forms, and DatePicker enables time editing through `time_precision`, `hour_cycle` and `default_time`. `date_time`/`set_date_time` expose the combined value; date/time presets use `DateRangePreset::date_time`. In time-enabled single mode, each edit emits Change while the popup stays open; range mode remains date-only. DatePicker Change payloads require migration below. ([#3206](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3206))  ## Window hosting and framework boundaries  - **One window entry point**: `gpui_kit::open_window(options, cx, build)` returns `(AnyWindowHandle, Entity<V>)` and always wraps application content in `gpui_base::Root`, independent of Cargo feature unification. Call `gpui_kit::init(cx)` first, return content from the builder, and retain the returned entity when you need application state. In async work, invoke the helper inside `cx.update`; quit/close actions and keybindings remain application-owned. ([#3152](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3152))  - **Automatic overlay hosting**: Root owns content, overlay hosting, keyboard traversal and selection copying; `gpui_component::Root` re-exports the Base type. Component initialization registers per-window presentation for dialogs, sheets, notifications, menus, tooltips, touch selection and window chrome. Manual layer-rendering APIs are removed; application operations remain on `WindowExt`. ([#3152](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3152))  - **Root plugins**: design systems can register typed `RootPlugin` implementations with `Root::register_plugin`, retrieve them with `Root::plugin`, and use preparation, styling and decoration hooks. Plugins are instantiated per window in registration order. Register them before creating windows; replacing a factory affects future windows, and existing roots are not retrofitted. ([#3152](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3152))  - **Plot primitives in Base**: scales, shapes, axes, grids, labels, the `Plot` trait, path caches and hover tracking move to `gpui_kit::base::plot`, enabling custom charts without a Component dependency. Existing `gpui_kit::component::plot` imports remain available through re-exports. `PlotElement<P>` is the element behind custom plots and `#[derive(IntoPlot)]`; `PlotMotion` and `PlotTheme` provide theme-projected pointer and hover motion. Base defaults to no motion, styled charts now accept `f32`, and Component forwards its `decimal` feature to Base. Custom primitive APIs require migration below. ([#3258](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3258))  ### Simplified window startup  `gpui_kit::init(cx)` and `gpui_kit::open_window` provide a compact, standard startup sequence. The builder creates application content, and Kit supplies the Root and automatically hosts dialogs, sheets and notifications. Use `WindowExt` for Component operations, `gpui_base::TextSelection` for selection, and `WindowOptions` with `window_border()` for window decoration. ([#3152](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3152))  rust gpui_kit::init(cx); let (window_handle, content) =     gpui_kit::open_window(options, cx, |window, cx| {         build_content(window, cx)     })?;   ### Updating existing window code  The following examples show how existing startup, overlay and window-operation code maps to the simplified APIs.  diff - cx.open_window(options, |window, cx| { -     let content = build_content(window, cx); -     cx.new(|cx| Root::new(content, window, cx)) - }) + let (window_handle, content) = +     gpui_kit::open_window(options, cx, |window, cx| { +         build_content(window, cx) +     })?;   diff - let sheet_layer = Root::render_sheet_layer(window, cx); - let dialog_layer = Root::render_dialog_layer(window, cx); - let notification_layer = Root::render_notification_layer(window, cx); -   div()       .child(content) -     .children(sheet_layer) -     .children(dialog_layer.map(|layer| deferred(layer).with_priority(1))) -     .children(notification_layer)   diff - Root::bordered - Root::window_shadow_size + window_border()   diff - window.selected_text(cx) + TextSelection::selected_text(window, cx) - window.has_text_selection(cx) + TextSelection::has_selection(window, cx) - root.clear_text_selection(window, cx) - window.clear_text_selection(cx) + TextSelection::clear(window, cx) - window.end_text_selection(cx) + TextSelection::end(window, cx)   diff - root.open_dialog(build, window, cx) + window.open_dialog(cx, build) - root.close_dialog(window, cx) + window.close_dialog(cx) - root.close_all_dialogs(window, cx) + window.close_all_dialogs(cx) - root.open_sheet_at(placement, build, window, cx) + window.open_sheet_at(placement, cx, build) - root.close_sheet(window, cx) + window.close_sheet(cx) - root.push_notification(notification, window, cx) + window.push_notification(notification, cx) - root.remove_notification::<T>(window, cx) + window.remove_notification::<T>(cx) - root.remove_notification1::<T>(key, window, cx) + window.remove_notification1::<T>(key, cx) - root.clear_notifications(window, cx) + window.clear_notifications(cx)   ## Editor and text input  - **Atomic inline tokens**: Input and Textarea can represent mentions, commands and resource references as `InlineToken` annotations. Use `replace_with_token`/`replace_range_with_token` to insert them, `InputContent::with_token` to restore validated ranges, and `content()` to save a draft. Caret movement, selection, deletion and undo treat each token as one unit, while values and clipboard content remain plain text. Customize presentation with `token` and activation with `on_token_click` or `ActivateToken`. Tokens are opt-in and unavailable in Editor, NumberInput, password and formatted-mask modes. ([#3113](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3113))  - **Editor range decorations**: independent consumers can create edit-tracked `RangeDecorationCollection` handles through `EditorState::create_range_decorations_collection`. `RangeDecoration` supports `RangeDecorationStyle::{Fill, Frame}` with optional color; collections can set, append, clear, read tracked ranges or dispose. Decorations use UTF-8 byte ranges and paint only visible spans, including soft wraps, CRLF and folded text. `InputExtras::range_decorations` has a default empty implementation, so existing implementers continue to compile. ([#3040](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3040))  - **Textarea sizing and editor gutters**: Textarea supports the standard size system like Input, and the line-number gutter adapts between three and seven digits instead of reserving an unsuitable fixed width. ([#3191](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3191), [#3205](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3205))  - **Focus, selection and asynchronous editing**: corrected disabled-input pointer focus, preferred columns and vertical selection affinity, drag-selection autoscroll, stale completion/inline responses, IME selection direction and undo boundaries, replacement-field focus and explicit GoToDefinition requests. Accessibility Focus refuses disabled inputs; SetValue is advertised and applied only when editable. Re-requesting code actions refreshes an already-open menu, including empty results. ([#3254](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3254), [#3256](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3256), [#3274](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3274))  - **Caret lifecycle and navigation**: programmatic updates no longer start permanent blink/repaint loops on unfocused inputs; blur clears stale blink state so refocusing restores a blinking caret. Editing scrolls directly to a distant caret rather than animating through the whole document. ([#3139](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3139), [#3140](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3140), [#3150](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3150))  - **Web clipboard paste**: Input context menus offer Paste whenever the input is editable, and fall back to asynchronous clipboard reading when synchronous reading is unavailable. The paste request starts from the user action and rechecks editability when it resolves. ([#3244](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3244))  - **Editor performance and Unicode safety**: reduce diagnostic/definition-hover repaints, debounce transient hover requests, avoid inactive search rescans and query only visible search highlights. Syntax classification reuses Rope-backed parse results and injection trees. Tree-sitter receives bytes, preventing aborts when a read begins inside a multibyte character. ([#3260](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3260), [#3263](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3263), [#3238](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3238))  - **Input context menus**: documentation now makes clear that `InputState::context_menu(false)` disables the entire right-click menu, including a custom Input/Textarea/Editor menu. Keep the state menu enabled when supplying a custom builder; the builder replaces the built-in items. ([#3275](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3275))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - **Source-aware selection**: `TextViewState::selected_source_range()` returns the original Markdown byte range for the actual rendered selection, distinguishing repeated occurrences instead of guessing from selected text. ([#3136](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3136))  - **Search highlights and reveal**: search `TextViewState::rendered_text()` and apply `RangeHighlight` values with `set_range_highlights`; `clear_range_highlights` removes them. Ranges refer to UTF-8 bytes in rendered copy text, so searches can cross Markdown formatting. Highlights preserve layout, selection and copy behavior, and survive unchanged content and append-only streaming. `reveal_range` makes the starting line visible in a scrollable TextView or an application list; `TextView::on_reveal` supplies window-space bounds for custom scroll containers. Reveal is best effort, and highlights are currently Markdown-only. ([#3215](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3215), [#3216](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3216))  - **Heading refinements**: Base `TextViewStyle::with_heading` accepts a level-aware `StyleRefinement` callback for typography, spacing, color and borders, layered over built-in heading defaults. Component keeps its existing compatibility heading fields and builders. ([#3129](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3129))  - **Markdown correctness**: preserve ordered-list starting numbers and prose in unclaimed math spans, pair inline HTML tags correctly, and keep lines containing inline code as tall as equivalent plain-text lines. ([#3204](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3204), [#3178](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3178), [#3240](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3240))  - **Streaming and large documents**: `stream_fade` staggers by word and skips oversized chunks, with throttled repaints. Reuse inline layout and paragraph render caches, avoid unnecessary hover/selection work, and remove quadratic source-mapping and table-highlight reconciliation paths. Text selection in a cached dock view stays registered instead of flickering and keeping the window repainting. ([#3153](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3153), [#3226](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3226), [#3180](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3180), [#3261](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3261), [#3247](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3247), [#3273](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3273), [#3193](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3193))  ## Charts and plot motion  - **Stable live-series geometry**: LineChart and AreaChart support `y_domain(min, max)` for a fixed value range and `point_count(count)` for reserved x-axis slots, so arriving data keeps its position. Values outside the fixed domain are clipped. BarChart adds `band_count` for the equivalent fixed-band layout. ([#3209](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3209), [#3220](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3220))  - **Axes, grids and reference lines**: LineChart/AreaChart add `y_axis`, `y_axis_label_placement`, `y_tick_count`, `y_tick_format`, `x_tick_count`, `grid_columns`, `grid_dashed`, repeatable `reference_line` and `y_padding`. BarChart adds `value_axis_label_placement`, `value_tick_format`, `band_tick_count` and `grid_dashed`. `AxisLabelPlacement::{Outside, Inside}` controls value-label placement. `chart.grid`/`ThemeColor::chart_grid` separate grids from axis borders, with a 60%-opacity border fallback for themes that omit the token. ([#3220](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3220), [#3236](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3236))  - **Bar labels and spacing**: `label_color` colors labels per datum, `padding_inner`/`padding_outer` expose band spacing, and `min_length` keeps zero or small bars visible. Vertical charts reserve space for labels. BarChart and CandlestickChart expose `max_band_width`, retaining their default 30px maximum while Base ScaleBand no longer imposes one. ([#3217](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3217), [#3258](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3258))  - **Tooltip customization**: LineChart, AreaChart, BarChart, RadarChart and CandlestickChart add `tooltip_title`, `tooltip_value`, `tooltip_value_color` and `tooltip_content`. Multivalue chart callbacks also receive a row index. PieChart and SankeyChart add `tooltip_name`/`tooltip_value` independently of labels painted on the chart. `Tooltip::value_color` colors the latest row and `plain_row` adds totals without a swatch; a bar tooltip swatch follows the bar color. ([#3228](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3228), [#3176](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3176))  - **Interaction and motion**: chart IDs default to their construction site so hover works without an explicit ID; provide distinct IDs when multiple charts share a construction site. `interactive(false)` removes hitboxes, hover emphasis, tooltips and path caches for placeholders or covered charts. Tooltip glides its own crosshair/dots and grows dot halos with hover progress; `Tooltip::glide(false)` disables that spring. Custom plots can use `PlotHover::glide` and the Base hover-motion helpers. ([#3173](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3173), [#3176](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3176), [#3222](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3222), [#3258](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3258))  - **Painting performance**: LineChart and AreaChart use index-based point placement to avoid quadratic value lookups and correctly position duplicate x values at their own indices. Candlestick wicks avoid per-candle path tessellation, and chart hover/painting avoids redundant work. ([#3262](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3262))  ## Dock and resizing  - **Nested docks and tab closing**: moving a panel from a dock that does not own it no longer creates duplicate or ghost tabs. `DockSkin::set_close_button_visible` enables built-in close buttons, hidden by default; `TabGroupContext::is_panel_closable` respects both dock and panel constraints. Custom tab renderers retain control over presentation and frame geometry. ([#3197](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3197))  - **Continuous bottom-dock resizing**: drag a collapsible bottom dock below its minimum, through the closed strip and back open in one motion. Release snaps to the nearer closed or minimum-open state; transient sizes below the minimum are not persisted. Custom resize drivers finish with `DockContext::end_resize`. ([#3229](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3229))  - **Resize indicators**: dividers grow their indicator as the pointer engages, hugging handles paint their hairline on the seam, and dock hairlines no longer draw over popovers. ([#3175](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3175), [#3200](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3200), [#3221](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3221))  ## Theme synchronization  - **Theme updates**: `Theme::update(cx, |theme| ...)` edits the global theme, reconciles solid colors and gradient tokens, updates the Base projection, resolves changed fonts and refreshes all windows. Use it for palette, mode, typography and other theme edits instead of manually synchronizing each representation. `Theme::global_mut` and `Theme::sync_base` remain available for callers that manage synchronization themselves. ([#3122](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3122))  ## Components and accessibility  - **Popover trigger styling**: `Popover::trigger_style` now applies to the actual trigger container, so width/flex refinements affect both parent layout and popup anchoring. Base Popover implements Styled; DropdownMenu keeps Button styling on the Button instead of applying it twice. ([#3245](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3245))  - **Attachment**: composer attachments include `on_remove`, `on_retry`, `progress(percent)` and `tooltip`; interactive controls and tooltips require `id`. Progress is 0–100 and clamped. Media receives correctly rounded corners and upload/failure treatments, including retry when a failed image has no source. `AttachmentGroup::track_scroll` lets the application drive scrolling and `with_edge_fade` signals clipped attachments. Default chip geometry and failure styling change to the composer presentation. ([#3195](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3195), [#3233](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3233))  - **Settings and GroupBox**: `GroupBox::footer`/`SettingGroup::footer` place supporting content outside the group surface. `SettingGroup::variant` overrides the `Settings::with_group_variant` default for one group; `GroupBoxVariant::Normal` presents items without a card surface. Selecting a group on another settings page now scrolls to the intended group. ([#3133](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3133), [#3203](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3203), [#3219](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3219))  - **Table**: `TableSelection::{None, Row, Column, Cell}` with `TableState::selection`/`set_selection` models the active selection as one value. Positional getters return only the active selection type; stale row appearance and accessibility selection state are corrected. Filler rows and cell rendering avoid redundant work. ([#3143](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3143), [#3264](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3264))  - **Popover**: `offset` sets the trigger gap and `arrow(true)` adds an arrow for all eight anchors. Horizontal-center anchor placement is corrected. Popover/AppMenu triggers use the new default `Selectable::open`/`is_open` methods to distinguish an open popup from application selection; existing Selectable implementations retain the default compatibility behavior. ([#3145](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3145), [#3125](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3125))  - **Dialog**: `button_props` merges explicitly supplied fields, preserving existing Cancel visibility and callbacks. AlertDialog adds `ok_text`, `ok_variant`, `cancel_text` and `cancel_variant`. Oversized dialogs reclaim the upper window space while normal and stacked dialogs retain their preferred placement. ([#3126](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3126), [#3188](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3188))  - **Select**: closing an open menu emits `DismissEvent`, including after Confirm; the popup aligns with the trigger at the right edge. Long option labels truncate with an ellipsis while keeping the checkmark visible. Custom full-row renderers receive a shrinkable container and should apply text truncation themselves. ([#3144](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3144), [#3218](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3218), [#3280](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3280))  - **Menus**: context-menu actions fire once on the row actually pressed even when rows share a construction site. PopupMenu, DropdownMenu and Popover release their entities and subscriptions when their host disappears, preventing leaks and stale popup registrations from suppressing later Input context menus. ([#3135](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3135), [#3224](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3224), [#3267](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3267), [#3279](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3279))  - **Switch**: the track shows a keyboard focus ring, with `focus_ring(false)`, `tab_stop` and `tab_index` controls. Long labels no longer shrink the track. ([#3120](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3120), [#3154](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3154))  - **Sidebar and List**: SidebarMenuItem exposes its visible label, TreeItem role and selected state to assistive technology. SidebarMenuItem, ListItem and SidebarToggleButton accept `accessibility_label`. The focused ListState container carries the List role, and a right-clicked list item retains its outline. ([#3160](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3160), [#3249](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3249), [#3155](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3155))  - **Chat composition**: `Marker::alignment(MarkerAlignment::{Start, Center, End})` controls row and wrapped-text alignment across variants. `Message::id`/`role` provide stable accessibility identity; Message content inherits caller/bubble typography. Ghost Bubble children are no longer clipped. ([#3151](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3151), [#3171](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3171), [#3172](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3172))  - **Form**: caller styling such as padding, gaps and background takes effect, and `Field::visible(false)` actually hides fields without leaving empty grid rows. Hidden fields are not built or laid out. ([#3248](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3248), [#3269](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3269))  ## Performance and resource use  - **Command**: unchanged command models retain their measured rows and search results across parent rerenders, while new callbacks and selection updates still apply. Models with custom child closures or changed layout continue to remeasure. ([#3268](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3268))  - **Tree and SearchableVec**: visible tree rows avoid deep subtree clones and defer context-menu entry cloning until the menu opens. SearchableVec filters by indices instead of cloning matching items or entire groups, evaluating each match once. ([#3265](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3265), [#3279](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3279))  - **Carousel and Rating**: ancestor scrolling no longer rebuilds Carousel geometry or interrupts a running snap animation. Rating repaints on real hover-value changes rather than every pointer movement. ([#3266](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3266), [#3270](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3270))  - **Pagination, ColorPicker, Accordion and Notification**: ellipsis menus show at most the 100 hidden pages nearest the current page; closed ColorPicker popups defer their bodies; Accordion content unmounts after the closing spring settles; persistent notifications stop lifecycle ticking once transitions finish. See the Accordion state-lifetime note below. ([#3279](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3279))  ## Shell, runtime and platform  - **GPUI snapshot**: Kit now pins the complete `gpui-pre-*` snapshot family to `=0.3.7`, including the newly published bench-metrics package. All 25 snapshot packages resolve together; the independent `gpui-pre-reqwest` fork remains at 0.12.15. ([#3283](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3283))  - **Shell inline tokens and TimeField**: JavaScript InputState/TextareaState mirror token insertion, draft content, token slots and token click callbacks. JavaScript ranges use UTF-16 offsets, while Rust uses UTF-8 bytes; validation failures expose `error.code`. Shell also exposes TimeField state/elements. Host integration adds optional retained-state methods via `StateMethodDescriptor`/`StateDescriptor::with_methods` and frame-owned interactive token subtrees. ([#3113](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3113), [#3206](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3206))  - **Script image policy**: HTML/Markdown TextView document images obey the creating script's HTTP GET capabilities, including intrinsic-size measurement and every redirect. Loading is asynchronous, view-owned and canceled on disposal, with timeout/body/redirect limits and no HTTPS downgrade. Non-HTTP(S) document images, including data URLs, are refused; the application-asset `image(path)` API is unchanged. Base adds a narrow document-image source override for embedders. ([#3230](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3230))  - **Base controls**: Base Dialog centers a normal-flow popup by default and blocks popup presses from reaching its backdrop; Base AlertDialog implements Styled. Base single-line Input vertically centers its text within its frame, while multiline layout is unchanged. These defaults do not alter styled Component Dialog/Input placement. ([#3277](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3277), [#3278](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3278))  - **Web and touch**: Base WASM examples bundle IBM Plex Sans so `.SystemUIFont` resolves and canvases render. Centered notification stacks work reliably on the web. A short touch drag that catches an active ScrollBounce no longer produces a reverse fling; intentional longer flings and edge bounce are preserved. ([#3235](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3235), [#3149](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3149), [#3207](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3207))  ## Documentation and website  - **Versioned documentation**: the latest release is served at `/`, development docs at `/versions/main`, and release snapshots at `/versions/<tag>`. Snapshot isolation and version-local links prevent newer pages or navigation from leaking into older docs; shared WASM examples load from the site root, and historical versions without component pages still build. ([#3159](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3159), [#3189](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3189), [#3243](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3243), [#3165](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3165), [#3196](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3196))  - **Framework manual**: expanded bilingual guides cover Entity, Window, Context, Render, RenderOnce, Element, Action/Event, ownership, focus, tasks, painting, text, accessibility, animation and WebAssembly, with runnable exercises and troubleshooting. New Packaging, Auto Update and Images guides cover distribution, user-level Linux installation, embedded assets, `img`, `svg` and HTTP caching. Menu-bar guidance documents binding keys before `set_menus`, and focus guidance explains explicit Tab stops. ([#3179](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3179), [#3181](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3181), [#3225](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3225), [#3232](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3232), [#3239](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3239), [#3124](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3124))  - **Website themes and navigation**: the theme palette reads theme JSON directly, previews and confirms with keyboard navigation, restores on cancel, and synchronizes the chosen JSON with embedded WASM examples. `Cmd+K`/`Ctrl+K` opens theme selection; `/` opens docs search. Text selection uses `selection.background`, falling back to `primary.background`, and active sidebar entries are easier to identify. Custom website themes and design-guide illustrations are refined. ([#3198](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3198), [#3241](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3241), [#3242](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3242), [#3237](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3237), [#3211](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3211), [#3212](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3212))  - **Documentation licensing**: eligible documentation prose and original illustrations carry CC BY 4.0 attribution; software and code examples retain Apache-2.0 permissions. ([#3225](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3225))  ## Breaking Changes  ### Custom Plot primitives  Component plot import paths remain re-exported, but custom plots must migrate primitive APIs. `StrokeStyle`/`stroke_style` are removed in favor of `Curve`/`curve`; scale ranges become arrays, domains accept iterators, and `PlotValue` replaces the hidden Sealed bound. `least_index` becomes `nearest_index`; `least_index_with_domain` is removed. Arc paints/contains use the radii configured on the Arc, and Grid x/y accept iterators. ScaleBand has no implicit 30px cap: set `max_band_width` if needed. `TooltipState`, `AxisText`, `label::Text`, `ArcData`, `StackPoint`, `StackSeries`, `SankeyLink`, `SankeyNodeLayout`, `SankeyLinkLayout` and `SankeyGraph` are non-exhaustive and should use their constructors. `PlotAxis::default()` now equals `new()` with its x-axis line visible. IntoPlot derives use `PlotElement<Self>` rather than implementing Element directly; the hidden `plot::tooltip::track_hover` is removed. Base Theme struct literals need `plot`. Deprecated aliases remain this release for `AXIS_GAP`, `dot_fill_color`, `dot_stroke_color`, `PlotHover::focus` and `Tooltip::focus`; prefer `axis_gutter`, `dot_fill`, `dot_stroke` and `progress`. Existing chart curve builders remain unchanged. ([#3258](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3258))  diff - use gpui_kit::base::plot::StrokeStyle; + use gpui_kit::base::plot::Curve; - Line::new().stroke_style(StrokeStyle::StepAfter) + Line::new().curve(Curve::StepAfter) - Area::new().stroke_style(StrokeStyle::Linear) + Area::new().curve(Curve::Linear)   diff - let y = ScaleLinear::new(values.collect(), vec![height, 0.]); + let y = ScaleLinear::new(values, [height, 0.]); - where Y: Copy + PartialOrd + Num + ToPrimitive + Sealed + where Y: PlotValue - let ix = scale.least_index(tick); + let ix = scale.nearest_index(tick); - arc.paint(&data, color, None, Some(radius), &bounds, window); + Arc::new().outer_radius(radius).paint(&data, color, &bounds, window); - Grid::new().y(ticks.collect()) + Grid::new().y(ticks)   diff   gpui_base::Theme {       appearance,       tokens,       scrollbar,       resizable, +     plot: gpui_base::PlotTheme::default(),   }   ### DatePicker event values  `DatePickerEvent::Change` now carries `DateTime`, not `Date`. Date-only consumers can use `value.date()`; `format` keeps its signature. Exhaustive matches on `DateRangePresetValue` must handle the new DateTime variant. ([#3206](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3206))  diff  cx.subscribe(&picker, |this, _, event, _| match event { -    DatePickerEvent::Change(date) => match date { +    DatePickerEvent::Change(value) => match value.date() {          Date::Single(Some(date)) => { /* … */ }          _ => {}      },  });   ### Base heading styling  Base `TextViewStyle` removes `with_heading_base_font_size`, `heading_base_font_size`, `with_heading_font_size` and `heading_font_size`. Use `with_heading`/`heading` and a StyleRefinement callback. The Component compatibility facade retains its old fields and builders. ([#3129](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3129))  diff -TextViewStyle::default() -    .with_heading_base_font_size(px(14.)) -    .with_heading_font_size(|level, base| resolve_size(level, base)) +TextViewStyle::default().with_heading(|level| { +    StyleRefinement::default().text_size(resolve_size(level)) +})   ### Table selection getters  Each positional getter reports only its active selection type. To obtain the row of a selected cell, map `selected_cell()` or match `selection()`; keep inactive-selection history in the application if required. ([#3143](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3143))  diff  table.set_selected_cell(5, 3, cx); -let row = table.selected_row(); +let row = table.selected_cell().map(|(row_ix, _)| row_ix);  table.set_selected_row(2, cx); -assert_eq!(table.selected_cell(), Some((5, 3))); +assert_eq!(table.selected_cell(), None); +assert_eq!(table.selection(), TableSelection::Row(2));   ### BarChart tick counts  `value_tick_count(n)` now means n ticks, not n intervals. Add one to an explicit old count to preserve its appearance. The default changes from four intervals to five ticks, preserving the default chart layout. ([#3209](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3209))  diff - BarChart::new(data).value_axis(true).value_tick_count(6) + BarChart::new(data).value_axis(true).value_tick_count(7)   ### Custom tooltip motion  Tooltip now springs its own crosshair/dots. Remove a custom spring or use `Tooltip::glide(false)` to avoid applying motion twice. Pass full Dot halo sizes; Tooltip applies hover progress itself. ([#3222](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3222))  diff - let center = self.band_center.unwrap_or(state.cross_line.x); - Tooltip::new(cursor, bounds.size) -     .cross_line(CrossLine::new(point(center, state.cross_line.y))) + Tooltip::new(cursor, bounds.size) +     .cross_line(CrossLine::new(state.cross_line))   diff - Dot::new(p).halo(px(20. * hover.focus())) + Dot::new(p).halo(px(20.))   ### Shell document image permissions  Script TextView signatures are unchanged, but remote document images now need explicit GET grants for their URL and every redirect target. Non-HTTP(S) sources, including data URLs, are refused; `image(path)` application assets are unaffected. ([#3230](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3230))  diff - Policy::new() + Policy::new().with_capabilities(Capabilities::new().http_requests([ +     HttpRequestGrant::new("images.example", ["GET"], ["/image.png"], [] as [&str; 0]), + ]))   ### Select dismissal ordering  Existing DismissEvent subscribers now run when an open menu closes. Confirm emits SelectEvent::Confirm followed by DismissEvent; opening or closing an already-closed menu does not dismiss. ([#3144](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3144))  diff  // An existing SelectState DismissEvent subscription after Escape: -// No callback. +// One callback after the menu transitions to closed.  // After confirming an item: -// SelectEvent::Confirm only. +// SelectEvent::Confirm, then DismissEvent.   ### Popover horizontal-center anchors  LeftCenter places the popup to the right of the trigger; RightCenter places it to the left, both vertically centered. These no longer fall back to top-left positioning. Top/bottom anchors keep their default placement and do not flip when clamped. ([#3145](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3145))  diff - .anchor(Anchor::LeftCenter)  // Legacy top-left fallback + .anchor(Anchor::LeftCenter)  // Right of the trigger, vertically centered - .anchor(Anchor::RightCenter) // Legacy top-left fallback + .anchor(Anchor::RightCenter) // Left of the trigger, vertically centered   ### Individually disabled Accordion items  An item marked disabled stays disabled instead of being accidentally enabled by its parent. Explicitly enable it if expansion is intended; builder signatures are unchanged. ([#3141](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3141))  diff  Accordion::new("settings").item(|item| item.title("Advanced").disabled(true)) -// Clicking Advanced can expand it. +// Clicking Advanced leaves it closed.   ### Message typography inheritance  Message no longer imposes text_sm and a 1.25 line height on its subtree. Header/footer typography remains local; bare MessageContent inherits the surrounding style, so explicitly style content that depended on the previous row defaults. ([#3171](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3171))  diff - Message::new().content(MessageContent::new().child("plain text"))   // rendered text_sm / 1.25 + Message::new().content(MessageContent::new().child("plain text"))   // inherits the caller's text style   ### Base Dialog placement and pointer occlusion  Normal-flow Base popups center by default; style the host to recover top-left placement. Popup presses no longer reach the backdrop, so size popup parts to their content instead of using a full-window popup wrapper. Styled Component Dialog positioning is unchanged. ([#3277](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3277))  diff - Dialog::new(cx).popup(popup) + Dialog::new(cx).items_start().justify_start().popup(popup)   ### Base single-line Input alignment  A Base single-line input fills its frame and vertically centers its line. If a tall frame relied on top placement, use a one-line-height frame for the previous appearance. Multiline and styled Component input layout is unchanged. ([#3278](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3278))  ### Accordion content lifetime and pagination limits  Closed Accordion content unmounts after its close spring settles, dropping element/keyed state held inside the panel. Preserve durable state in entities. Under reduced motion this optimization keeps content mounted. Pagination ellipsis menus cap hidden-page choices at the nearest 100; use successive menus to reach farther pages. ([#3279](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3279))  ### Form visibility and attachment presentation  `Field::visible(false)` now hides the field instead of being ignored. Attachment has new default chip sizing, radius, status overlays and failure colors; review applications that depended on its former content-sized geometry. ([#3269](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3269), [#3195](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3195))  ### Chart labels and duplicate x values  LineChart/AreaChart position duplicate x values at their individual indices, matching hover. Vertical BarChart with labels reserves slightly more top space (bars draw 2px shorter); PieChart tooltip labels are configured separately from leader-line labels. ([#3262](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3262), [#3217](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3217), [#3176](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3176))  Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback! ## What's Changed * chore: Add a /release-notes command by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3117 * chart: Start a pie leader line at the lifted slice edge by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3121 * chore: Move the tested consumer crate into the workspace by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3118 * chart: Fix the pie radius fallback and fit the gallery's region labels by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3123 * docs: # Changelog  ## ✨ 新增功能 - 申请 ZeroSSL 证书时支持自动获取 EAB 凭据：EAB KID 与 HMAC Key 留空时，将使用证书联系邮箱自动向 ZeroSSL 获取 - 证书列表新增「主题名称（CN）」与「主题备用名称（SAN）」两列，SAN 列最多展示 3 个域名，超出部分以 +N 显示 - 证书详情中的主题备用名称改为以标签形式逐个展示  ## 🐛 修复 Bug - 修复 #1461：部署证书到火山引擎 WAF（CNAME 接入模式）时因缺少源站配置导致 InvalidParameter 错误 - 修复部署证书到 LeCDN v4 时 API 认证失败及证书部署异常的问题  ## 🛠 优化 & 其他改动 - 代码清理与重构（LeCDN、火山引擎 WAF 部署提供商）  ---  Comparing changes: https://github.com/certimate-go/certimate/compare/v0.4.33...v0.4.34, by @asd80703406, @fudiwei, @usual2970, @Yeqingky
```

### 2026-09-23T12:47:47Z [**OneOhCloud/OneBox**](https://github.com/OneOhCloud/OneBox)

```
# Changelog  Version 1.4.43 (2026-09-23 UTC)  ## Changes  - Fixed mainland China websites failing to open in Windows TUN mode on some IPv6 networks. - OneBox automatically selects a working IPv6 connection without waiting for unavailable connections during startup, and temporarily uses IPv4 when IPv6 is unavailable. - The config viewer now shows the config currently in use. - Updated JavaScript and Rust dependencies.  ## 变更  - 修复部分 IPv6 网络下 Windows 开启 TUN 后无法访问中国大陆网站的问题。 - 自动选择可用的 IPv6 连接，启动时无需等待不可用连接超时，IPv6 不可用时临时使用 IPv4。 - 配置查看器现在显示实际生效的配置。 - 更新 JavaScript 与 Rust 依赖。 
```

```
2.x is a rewrite and is not compatible with 1.x data, start 2.x from an empty database and a new `encryption.key`.  ---  2.x 是全新重写版本，与 1.x 数据不兼容，请以全新数据库和新的 `encryption.key` 部署 2.x。   ## What's Changed * fix(execution): preserve configured extensions during protocol conversion by @tbphp in https://github.com/tbphp/gpt-load/pull/788 * chore(codex): upgrade sdk and codex model support by @tbphp in https://github.com/tbphp/gpt-load/pull/791   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/tbphp/gpt-load/compare/v2.0.0-rc.37...v2.0.0-rc.38
```

### 2026-09-24T15:17:34Z [**cokice/japanese-analyzer**](https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer)

```
本次发布更新了日语阅读与解析界面，新增多语言界面、解析历史和多词短语解析，并修复流式响应及上游超时处理问题。  ## 新功能与体验改进  - 支持简体中文、繁体中文、English、한국어，界面、翻译和释义随语言切换。 - 重新设计首页与阅读视图；解析时保留待解析原文，阅读工具栏保持可访问。 - 支持选择多个词并作为短语解析，也可合并被拆开的词项。 - 新增浏览器本地解析历史，方便重新打开最近输入。 - 改进网页和 Markdown 文本粘贴，更新多语言 README、截图及 AI Agent 部署指南。  ## 修复与性能改进  - 将 Next.js 和 eslint-config-next 升级至 15.5.26。 - 翻译和图片识别流式响应缺少正常结束信号时报告错误，避免把截断内容当作成功结果。 - 为上游错误响应正文增加读取超时及大小限制。 - 改用按 Unicode 范围分片的日文字体，减少不必要的字体下载。 - 修复短语选择与释义字段、每日一句时效性及输入框尺寸问题。 - 排除本地工具缓存目录，避免污染 ESLint 检查。  ## 许可证  当前版本采用 AGPL-3.0-only；mit-final 标签及此前代码保留原 MIT 授权。详见仓库 LICENSE、LICENSING.md 与 NOTICE。  ## 验证  发布提交：df7524683af2763ff2f09657b4646b2bd200b8d0。该提交的 CI 测试、ESLint 与 Next.js 生产构建均已通过。  完整变更：https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer/compare/v0.3.0...v0.4.0 
```

### 2026-09-27T21:27:34Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - New option "quality of resized images" (`imageQuality`, 0.8 by default): the quality used to re-encode the JPEG and WEBP images resized with "image reduction factor". With 1, WEBP images are saved without loss. The field is disabled while the image reduction factor is 1 - The option "compress CSS content" is removed. It has had no effect since single-file-core 1.6.15: on 24 real pages (38.4 MB of CSS), it took 3.4 seconds to save 0.015% (5,850 bytes), made 6 of them larger, and could move background images (`background:none` became `background:0`) - The help of "image reduction factor" now explains that images whose size is not set by the page (e.g. icons, logos) are displayed smaller - single-file-core is updated from 1.6.14 to 1.6.19, see https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.15, https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.16, https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.17, https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.18 and https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.19  ### Fixes  - A font stylesheet served only with the page's `Referer` (e.g. the Sentinel fonts of authorsguild.org) was dropped from the saved page. When the fetch of a `<link>` stylesheet fails, the rules loaded by the page are now used instead - The `referrerpolicy` attribute of `<img>`, `<source>`, `<link>` and `<script>` elements is now applied when fetching them, and no `Referer` is sent for a resource of a stylesheet whose `Referrer-Policy` gives none - With "image reduction factor" set, a resized image is no longer kept when it is larger than the original (e.g. small icons and avatars), the images of `<canvas>` elements are resized too, the images of `background` attributes are no longer resized, and an SVG `<image>` without size keeps its displayed size - When the encoding of a resized image failed, the save never ended - A page whose content is an exact multiple of 16 MiB (e.g. 32 MiB) was never saved, and a page of an exact multiple of 32 MiB never opened in the annotation editor (#2004) - A shadow root created with `delegatesFocus` or with manual slot assignment was not restored as such in the saved page - The `<source>` elements and the `environmentmap` attribute of `<model>` elements are now embedded - Resources with no expected type (e.g. a PDF in an `<object>`) were requested with the header `Accept: undefined` - The removal of unused styles removed vendor values the browser does not support (e.g. `-moz-` values in Chrome), fallback values written before a newer value, the values `revert-rule` rolls back to, the rules in `@when` / `@else`, and could lower the specificity of nested rules - A `data-single-file-stylesheet` attribute was left on some `<style>` elements of the page after saving it 
```

```
## Release Notes  Released on 2026-09-24.  ### Preview features  - \[`ruff`\] Avoid false positives for overloaded division (`RUF069`) ([#28309](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28309))  ### Bug fixes  - \[`flake8-bugbear`\] Avoid false positives for calls with keyword arguments (`B009`, `B010`, `B043`) ([#28776](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28776)) - \[`flake8-tidy-imports`\] Allow lazy imports to be used in deferred annotations (`TID255`) ([#28767](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28767))  ### Rule changes  - Update LibCST-based fixes for Python 3.15 ([#28616](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28616)) - \[`flake8-pyi`\] Mention stubs in the diagnostic message (`PYI002`) ([#28542](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28542))  ### Documentation  - Fix horizontal overflow on the rules documentation page ([#28699](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28699)) - Update rules table with category information ([#28651](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28651)) - \[`flake8-annotations`\] Clarify that `ANN401` checks return types in addition to arguments ([#28334](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28334)) - \[`flake8-bugbear`\] Document type-checker interaction (`B010`) ([#28509](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28509)) - cleanly](https://github.com/Xpra-org/xpra/commit/2789cdf5ec5c3350237f4a2689ab1b2ef91ea70b)   * [OpenGL scrolling no longer clips updates or reuses stale
```
