- Added a thumbnail filmstrip for quickly previewing and navigating nearby photos and videos in the gallery viewer. - Added support for sharing photos from person pages. - Set photos as your home screen, lock screen, or both wallpapers on Android, with a preview to adjust the crop. - Added press and hold 2x playback for videos (@fosszil) - Gallery scrolling performance improvements. - Made back buttons easier to tap without changing their appearance or spacing. - Fixed device storage buildup caused by temporary Live Photo videos left behind during backup on iOS. - Create albums from unmatched album searches. - Added Slideshow to on-device albums. - Added longer slideshow duration options. - Show archived albums in Albums search results. - Show person-aware avatars in the file details "Added by" row when the uploader resolves to a person. - Updated the People menu to match the current Photos interface. - Updated the person cover photo picker to match the current Photos interface. - Updated the person picker app bar and sort menu, and fixed overflowing person names. - Fixed uploaded photos appearing as not uploaded after signing back in on iOS. - Fixed panoramas appearing upside down on some Android devices. - Fixed image and video viewer status bar styles. (@r4khul) - Show load errors for Referrals and Cast sessions.

适配 7.1 新角色，自动剧情语音检测优化等。  ### 自动战斗  - 优化实验性自动连招的战技冷却判断与站场动作，支持技能打断普攻，并在技能空窗期执行兜底攻击 (#3679 #3691) @FishmanTheMurloc - 战斗策略匹配人数相同时优先选择匹配比例更高的策略，避免通用策略覆盖专用策略 (#3698) @zmylol - 修复万叶、琴战后拾取时可能未切换到对应角色的问题 (#3689) @this-Fish  ### 地图追踪  - 修复万叶拾取配置无法正确生效的问题 (#3579) @Bedrockx - 扩大大地图左上角界面遮挡区域的点击避让范围，减少传送误点 (#3688) @1004452714  ### JS脚本  - 背包物品计数支持自动切换所属页面和按物品排序提前结束扫描，结果统一返回物品数量映射 (#3657 #3705) @1004452714 - 移除受 10 MB 限制的 GitCode 脚本仓库更新源  ### 其他  - 一条龙首领讨伐：可分别设置单次及跨运行的成功领奖上限，并可清空累计次数 (#3683) @ShadowLemoon - 自动秘境：至冬秘境交互项改用文字识别选择，并等待交互生效，减少误选附近造物 - 自动剧情：修正对话选项语音检测的 Silero VAD 前处理，新增实时诊断面板与录音保存 (#3694) @ShadowLemoon - 物品图标识别默认使用 ItemV2，并支持在设置中切换模型，覆盖钓鱼、自动吃药、合成和奖励识别等场景 (#3644) @1004452714 - 界面多语言：新增繁体中文动态翻译（关联 #3674） - 至冬枪械操作，检测到预设二时给出调整提示，避免滚轮绑定影响部分脚本及战斗结束检测

## v2.5.6  <details> <summary><strong> 🐞 修复问题 </strong></summary>  - 修复「代理集合」「规则集合」订阅响应较慢时更新误报失败、且不显示真实失败原因的问题  **🖥️ Windows**  - 修复 Windows 部分未安装服务的用户以普通权限运行时，内核无法启动的问题 - 修复 Windows 系统隔离权限被误判，导致服务模式和 TUN 无法使用的问题 - 修复 Windows 旧版服务状态残留导致无法重装的问题，自动备份可识别的残留并恢复安装 - 修复 Windows 服务已停止时，启动要等约两分钟才显示窗口的问题 - 优化 Windows 服务安全检查失败提示：用易懂的文字说明原因，并提供修复文档链接  **🍎 macOS**  - macOS 修复编辑规则时「规则内容」输入的首字母被自动转为大写的问题 - macOS 修复残留的服务进程导致内核无法启动、「继续使用 Sidecar」失败且无法修复的问题 - macOS 修复部分账户安装或修复服务时提示无法解析用户组而失败的问题 - macOS 优化用户目录在外置磁盘时服务拒绝启动内核的提示：说明原因并给出修复命令 - macOS 修复服务模式内核意外停止后，系统代理仍指向失效端口导致无法上网的问题  </details>  <details> <summary><strong> 🚀 优化改进 </strong></summary>  - 优化内核启动失败和服务模式内核意外停止的提示：显示具体原因，并在窗口恢复后提示尚未解决的错误  </details>  ## 下载地址  ### Windows (不再支持Win7) #### 正常版本(推荐) - 本月概要： * 【OA】完成 OA 协同办公模块整合，补齐对应的后端工程与业务能力，并支持 Admin 移动端。 * 【PMS】完成项目管理模块整合，补齐对应的后端工程与业务能力，并支持 Admin 移动端。 * 【稳定性】修复工作流、AI、IoT、CRM、ERP、FMS、IM 及前端管理后台等模块的用户反馈问题，增强系统稳定性。  ### 📈 Statistic  * 总代码行数：647011 * 源码代码行数：416656 * 注释行数：135879 * 单元测试用例数：4285  ### 🚀 新增功能  后端框架专区： * 【新增】增加 OceanBase MySQL 模式的接入支持，提供 Docker 测试环境、初始化 SQL 及 SQL 同步生成入口；验证基础 JDBC 操作与 Flowable 引擎的建表、部署、审批等流程，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [00680ddc1b](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/ruoyi-vue-pro/commit/00680ddc1b5ab9c035b2c0392378388d96112e1d)，对应国产数据库适配诉求 [IK3MDW](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/yudao-cloud/issues/IK3MDW)，使用方式见 [《国产信创数据库》](/xinchuang-db) * 【新增】增加文件上传类型白名单校验，后端上传根据文件内容识别类型，前端直传校验文件名类型，支持 SVG 等常用图片、文档、压缩包和音视频，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [03ce4fad57](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/ruoyi-vue-pro/commit/03ce4fad5766bd85d318b9d6fb4cf8e43dad92d1)，对应 [#270](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/yudao-cloud/pulls/270) * 【新增】增加超级管理员、租户管理员账号保护，禁止禁用及单个、批量删除管理员账号，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [223a4d816](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/ruoyi-vue-pro/commit/223a4d816aa1c198dbc3b8e252700e183ee709a1)，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/MbI7r>  OA 协同办公专区： * 【新增】日常协作：支持日程、任务、工作计划、工作汇报、公告、讨论、通讯录和笔记，覆盖参与人共享、任务反馈、公告阅读、讨论互动及内容分享；由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [单体后端 09cf3017e1](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/ruoyi-vue-pro/commit/09cf3017e19fcf8dd17ee0993f2cc989f27a9fd9)、[Vue3 b2c0d339d](https://gitee.com/yudaocode/yudao-ui-admin-vue3/commit/b2c0d339d) * 【新增】办公服务：支持企业邮箱和企业云盘，提供企业服务、个人账号、文件夹、邮件、目录、共享权限及收藏等功能；同步 Vue3、Vben5.0 和 UniApp 管理后台 * 【新增】行政管理：支持办公用品、用印、会议室和车辆管理，覆盖用品领用发放与归还、印章申请、会议室预定、用车和还车申请 * 【新增】流程申请：支持公文、出差、费用报销、请假、加班、转正和离职申请，支持保存草稿后提交 BPM 本月概要： * 【OA】完成 OA 协同办公模块整合，补齐对应的后端工程与业务能力，并支持 Admin 移动端。 * 【PMS】完成项目管理模块整合，补齐对应的后端工程与业务能力，并支持 Admin 移动端。 * 【稳定性】修复工作流、AI、IoT、CRM、ERP、FMS、IM 及前端管理后台等模块的用户反馈问题，增强系统稳定性。  ### 📈 Statistic  * 总代码行数：647011 * 源码代码行数：416656 * 注释行数：135879 * 单元测试用例数：4285  ### 🚀 新增功能  后端框架专区： * 【新增】增加 OceanBase MySQL 模式的接入支持，提供 Docker 测试环境、初始化 SQL 及 SQL 同步生成入口；验证基础 JDBC 操作与 Flowable 引擎的建表、部署、审批等流程，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [00680ddc1b](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/ruoyi-vue-pro/commit/00680ddc1b5ab9c035b2c0392378388d96112e1d)，对应国产数据库适配诉求 【新增】办公服务：支持企业邮箱和企业云盘，提供企业服务、个人账号、文件夹、邮件、目录、共享权限及收藏等功能；同步 Vue3、Vben5.0 和 UniApp 管理后台 * 【新增】行政管理：支持办公用品、用印、会议室和车辆管理，覆盖用品领用发放与归还、印章申请、会议室预定、用车和还车申请 * 【新增】流程申请：支持公文、出差、费用报销、请假、加班、转正和离职申请，支持保存草稿后提交 BPM 审批，并按业务类型关联出差、用品、会议室和车辆等后续操作；同时支持 UniApp 移动端 OA，覆盖 H5、微信小程序等多端，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [3e28510ce](https://gitee.com/yudaocode/yudao-ui-admin-uniapp/commit/3e28510ce)  PMS 项目管理专区： * 【新增】项目中心：支持项目、成员、分组、收藏、公告、模板、迭代、工作项、状态、看板、标签、评论、动态和工时，覆盖项目从创建、规划到执行的协作流程；由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [单体后端 31aded61d8](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya/ruoyi-vue-pro/commit/31aded61d8)、[Vue3 2e0019924](https://gitee.com/yudaocode/yudao-ui-admin-vue3/commit/2e0019924) * 【新增】知识中心：支持知识库、文件夹、文档、模板、文档权限与协作，覆盖文档创建、共享、收藏、回收站和最近使用等场景，并支持 UniApp 移动端；由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 完善 [Vben5.0 ### 2026-09-25T01:13:36Z [**tw93/Pake**](https://github.com/tw93/Pake)

```
<div align="center"> <img src="https://gw.alipayobjects.com/zos/k/fa/logo-modified.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.17.2</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog  1. Linux apps on WebKitGTK 2.52 or later keep hardware acceleration on both X11 and Wayland, so videos play without crashing and fullscreen keeps the player controls. 2. Windows opened with `--multi-window` and pop-up windows get the same permissions as the main window, so external links, downloads, and notifications work there too. 3. GNOME and other Linux docks match a running app to its launcher, showing the right icon and keeping pinned entries working. 4. niri sessions get native WebKit rendering back, which the 3.17.0 flag change had dropped. 5. Grok quote cards stay inside the content column when the author name is long. 6. `--windows-toolchain gnu` builds keep the required linker flags when `CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS` is set.  ### 更新日志  1. WebKitGTK 2.52 及以上的 Linux 应用在 X11 和 Wayland 下都保留硬件加速，播放视频不再崩溃，全屏时播放器控件也还在。 2. `--multi-window` 打开的窗口和弹出窗口拥有和主窗口一样的权限，外部链接、下载和通知在这些窗口里也能正常用。 3. GNOME 等 Linux 程序坞能把运行中的应用和启动器对上，图标显示正确，固定到程序坞的入口也能正常用。 4. niri 会话重新用上 WebKit 原生渲染，这是 3.17.0 调整参数时丢掉的。 5. 作者名较长时，Grok 的引用卡片不再超出内容栏。 6. 设置了 `CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS` 时，`--windows-toolchain gnu` 构建也会保留必需的链接参数。  Special thanks to @Yaman-Harfoush, @mestadler, and @x0rzavi for the reports, and to @drakeo338, @yhcharles, and @houzixiashanxiedaima for the contributions behind this release. If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.  > https://github.com/tw93/Pake 
```

[English](#en-v2.3.2) | [中文](#cn-v2.3.2)  <h3 id="en-v2.3.2">New Features</h3>  * Panel Settings: Added support for creating multiple API keys by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13872 * Virtual Machine Management: Added support for mounting multiple CD-ROM ISO images by @ssongliu  ### Enhancements  * Overview: Improved logging for disk command failures by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13889 * File Browser: Improved task messages for file copy, move, compress, and decompress by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13885 * Firewall: Optimized firewall state reads and batch verification logic by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13881 * System: Removed the minimum upgrade backup copies limit by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13907  ### Bug Fixes  * Models: Fixed the Ollama page failing to open properly in some scenarios by @zhengkunwang223 in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13855 * Models: Fixed vLLM uninstall failures in some scenarios by @zhengkunwang223 * App Store: Fixed application upgrade failures in some scenarios by @zhengkunwang223 in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13888 * Websites: Fixed incorrect priority between HTTP-to-HTTPS redirects and website redirects by @A-Words in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13847 * Certificates: Fixed auto-renewing certificates still triggering expiry alerts by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13895 * File Browser: Fixed file rename failures in some scenarios by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13891 * Firewall: Fixed IPv6 forwarding being disrupted by RA by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13882 * Firewall: Fixed incomplete batch operation and task logs by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13886 * Firewall: Fixed incorrect reporting of port allowances and whitelist synchronization by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13892 * Firewall: Fixed rule synchronization failures in some firewalld scenarios by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13896 * Log Audit: Fixed SSH login logs not being displayed in the server timezone by @wanghe-fit2cloud in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13869 * Virtual Machine Management: Fixed abnormal display of some virtual machine states and the inability to wake sleeping virtual machines by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13890 * Virtual Machine Management: Fixed incorrect CPU topology settings for virtual machines by @ssongliu  <h3 id="cn-v2.3.2">新增功能</h3>  * 【面板设置】支持创建多个 API Key by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13872 * 【虚拟机管理】支持挂载多个 CD-ROM ISO 镜像 by @ssongliu  ### 功能优化  * 【概览】完善磁盘命令执行失败时的日志记录 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13889 * 【文件】完善文件复制、移动、压缩和解压任务的消息提示 by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13885 * 【防火墙】优化防火墙状态读取及批量校验逻辑 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13881 * 【系统】移除最低升级备份副本数限制 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13907  ### 问题修复  * 【模型】修复了部分场景下 Ollama 页面无法正常打开的问题 by @zhengkunwang223 in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13855 * 【模型】修复了部分场景下 vLLM 卸载失败的问题 by @zhengkunwang223 * 【应用商店】修复了部分场景下应用升级失败的问题 by @zhengkunwang223 in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13888 * 【网站】修复了 HTTP 跳转 HTTPS 与网站重定向优先级异常的问题 by @A-Words in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13847 * 【证书】修复了自动续签证书仍触发到期告警的问题 by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13895 * 【文件】修复了文件重命名操作在部分场景下异常的问题 by @lan-yonghui in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13891 * 【防火墙】修复了 IPv6 转发受 RA 干扰的问题 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13882 * 【防火墙】修复了批量操作与任务日志显示不完整的问题 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13886 * 【防火墙】修复了端口放行与白名单同步上报异常的问题 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13892 * 【防火墙】修复了 firewalld 环境下部分场景规则同步异常的问题 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13896 * 【日志审计】修复了 SSH 登录日志未按服务器时区显示的问题 by @wanghe-fit2cloud in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13869 * 【虚拟机管理】修复了部分虚拟机状态显示异常及休眠虚拟机无法唤醒的问题 by @ssongliu in https://github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/pull/13890 * 【虚拟机管理】修复了虚拟机 CPU 拓扑设置不正确的问题 by @ssongliu

### 2026-09-24T15:31:15Z [**zurawiki/gptcommit**](https://github.com/zurawiki/gptcommit)

```
### Upgrade notes  - Building from source now requires Rust 1.88 or newer. - `file_ignore` now uses Git-style patterns instead of substring matching. Use   `*.lock` for a filename suffix, `/path` for a repository-root path, and ordered   `!` rules to include exceptions. Review custom rules when upgrading; for   example, a substring rule `lock` should become `*.lock` to exclude lockfiles.   These rules are separate from the repository's `.gitignore`. - The default OpenAI model is now `gpt-6-luna`, with low reasoning effort and a   2,048-token limit shared by reasoning and output for each request. Explicit model   overrides are preserved and do not receive these model-specific options.   Choose another model with `gptcommit config set openai.model <model>` if needed.  ### Improvements  - Configuration reads display booleans, numbers, and arrays correctly. Setting or   deleting a key edits only that key in the selected file, preserves comments,   and avoids copying environment or unrelated configuration overrides into it. - Ignore rules support globs, directories, anchored paths, and negation. Verbose   output identifies excluded files and the rules that matched them. - Empty or fully ignored changes leave the commit message untouched and make no   API calls. Routine progress output is quieter; use `--verbose` for details. - File summarization runs at most four requests concurrently, preserves stable   output order, and reports failures instead of silently omitting summaries. - Default prompts request concise, factual titles and bullets without invented   motivation or test claims. Bun and uv lockfiles are excluded by default. - OpenAI client and dependency updates improve compatibility and retry handling;   errors are reported without panics, with tokenization fallback for unknown models.  ### Release maintenance  - Updated GitHub Actions are pinned to commit hashes. - Homebrew and Winget updates wait until all release binaries are uploaded,   removing the race with archive downloads.  [0.6.0]: https://github.com/zurawiki/gptcommit/compare/v0.5.17...v0.6.0 
```

✨新增 - `直播首页暂停` 新增隐藏首页直播板块的选项. (PR #5744 by [YiJay99](https://github.com/YiJay99)) - `动态过滤` 的板块开关支持键盘操作. (PR #5793 by [grcOvO](https://github.com/koagaroon))  🐛修复 - 修复 `定制自动连播行为` 在普通分 P 新版界面失效, 以及 `传统连播模式` 在多 P 视频中无法按设置连播的问题. (#5642, PR #5758 by [dot-super](https://github.com/dot-super)) - 修复 `隐藏顶部横幅` 在新版顶栏出现首页顶部全白及深色模式文字颜色不正确的问题. (#5496, PR #5800 by [T0M](https://github.com/T0MYYY)) - 修复 `删除广告` 导致首页推荐流空位、新版合集列表底部空白, 以及搜索页广告未隐藏的问题. (#5699, #5756, PR #5753, PR #5803 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `隐藏热搜` 失效, 并在空关键词搜索时打开搜索页. (#5364, PR #5802 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `评论图片导出` 的来源视频或动态 ID 解析和压缩包命名问题. (PR #5801 by [塵埃殘憶](https://github.com/kaixinol)) - 修复 `深色模式` 在直播中心、漫画、blackboard 等页面的适配问题, 并排除不应运行脚本的页面. (PR #5763, PR #5772 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `播放时自动关灯` 触发页面自动刷新、星光动画不同步, 以及 `播放器位置动作` 导致页面闪烁和评论区跳动的问题. (#5125, #5269, #5463, PR #5757 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `自动收起直播侧栏` 在网页打开时弹出侧栏的问题. (PR #5754 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `直播首页静音` 不适配新版首页布局的问题. (PR #5791 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `下载字幕` 将无字幕分 P 计为成功并生成空条目的问题. (PR #5770 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `一键点亮直播间粉丝勋章` 将点赞发送到佩戴勋章对应直播间的问题, 并支持一键填入当前或佩戴勋章的直播间 ID. (#5761, PR #5762 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `直播弹幕发送栏` 输入中断及输入框自动填充问题. (#5301, #2994, PR #5779 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `动态分组过滤` 的默认分组判断和深色模式样式问题. (#5345, PR #5751 by [Skalo](https://github.com/KingLou4329)) - 修复 `自定义顶栏` 遇到不可见专栏卡片时报错、导航栏项目出现滚动条, 以及漫画封面使用非 HTTPS 地址的问题. (#5646, #5760, #2888, PR #5767 by [Uchida Kotori](https://github.com/PriceHu), PR #5778 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55), PR #5792 by [grcOvO](https://github.com/koagaroon)) - 修复官方深色模式下 Toast 卡片关闭按钮与进度环颜色异常, 以及活动直播间覆盖管理面板样式的问题. (PR #5750, PR #5775 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `弹幕转义` 对高级弹幕不生效的问题. (#5745, PR #5749 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复临时加速污染倍速切换记忆的问题. (#5710, PR #5743 by [yousizaitianqiong](https://github.com/yousizaitianqiong)) - 修复 `播放器控制栏背景色` 在课堂、活动和番剧页面的高度与位置适配问题. (#4260, PR #5774 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `禁止跳转动态详情` 等动态相关功能在个人空间搜索动态页失效的问题. ### 2026-09-29T17:44:34Z [**PowerJob/PowerJob**](https://github.com/PowerJob/PowerJob)

```
## 中文  - 恢复 5.1.x 的升级兼容行为，修正旧日志下载链接、实例详情及容器部署的兼容问题，恢复既有 Client 传输和权限配置行为。 - 修复提前查看暂无日志的实例后，占位提示可能被归档并覆盖后续真实日志的问题；已有占位缓存可在原始日志仍可用时重新读取。 - 修复请求缓存过滤器对带 boundary/charset 的 Content-Type 识别，保持 multipart 上传字节完整。 - 将 Fastjson 从 1.2.83 更新至兼容的 1.2.84，保留 1.x API。  继续支持 Java 8，无数据库 schema 迁移。Server 可单独升级，无需旧 Client、Worker 或 Console 同步升级；保留既有登录与鉴权开关。历史原始日志已丢失时，本次修复无法恢复其内容。 若自定义登录扩展使用了 5.1.4/5.1.5 新增的 `PowerJobLoginService.ifLogin(String)`，请改回原 `ifLogin(HttpServletRequest)` 接口。  ## English  - Restore 5.1.x upgrade compatibility for legacy log downloads, instance details and container deployment, together with existing Client transport and permission configuration behavior. - Fix early log reads caching a no-log placeholder as the final archive and overwriting later logs. Existing placeholder caches can be refreshed when the original logs remain available. - Correct Content-Type handling with boundary/charset parameters in the request cache filter, preserving multipart upload bytes. - Update Fastjson from 1.2.83 to the compatible 1.2.84 release while retaining the 1.x API.  Java 8 remains supported, with no database schema migration. Servers can be upgraded independently of existing Clients, Workers and Consoles; existing login and authentication switches remain available. Previously lost original log data cannot be recovered by this fix. Custom login extensions using the `PowerJobLoginService.ifLogin(String)` overload introduced in 5.1.4/5.1.5 must return to the original `ifLogin(HttpServletRequest)` interface.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/PowerJob/PowerJob/compare/v5.1.5...v5.1.6 
```

### 2026-09-27T15:50:43Z [**floccusaddon/floccus**](https://github.com/floccusaddon/floccus)

```
 ### Summary - Over 60 small and larger performance and correctness fixes, especially for Android and iOS. - Support for tags on Android and iOS, for adapters that support them. - Git support on Android and iOS  ### New * [native] feat: Support tags on Android and iOS * [native] feat: Display tags of the current folder in a sheet below the top bar * [native] feat: Allow searching for multiple tags and allow matching tags by searches partially * [native] feat(git): Make git work on native * [native] feat: Don't let screen go dark during sync  ### Fixed * fix(OAuth): Prompt people to re-login if they do cross-device imports of OAuth profiles * [native] perf: Implement SQLite-backed tree storage * [native] perf: Move logs to sqlite * [native] perf: Move cache tree to sqlite * [native] perf(sqlite): Flush in a microtask instead of using setTimeout * [native] perf: Actually use NativeTree#bulkImportFolder during sync * perf: Use a global iterations counter for serializing the tree asynchronously to give the runtime breathing room * perf: Remove unnecessary Parallel.map(.., 1) call * perf: Optimize Diff usage * perf: Optimize cache persistence * perf: Scale progressInterval with tree size * perf(NextcloudBookmarks): Do not load all bookmarks just to delete a bookmark * perf: Track pending continuation in SQLite/IndexedDB * perf: Simple speed up of continuation serialization * perf(tree): Avoid rebuilding index on every mutation * [native] fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix bulkImport * [native] fix: Introduce preferences setters * [native] refactor: Make UI read from sqlite * [native] refactor: Keep & invalidate folder hashes rather than recomputing them * [native] fix: Update Podfile and cap config * [native] fix(tags): Sort tags in normalizeTags * [native] fix(tests): Use different URLs for each test and fix tags convergence * [native] tests(tags): Add tags tests to Appium test suite * [native] fix: Make sure search items are updated when the tree changes * fix(ui): Fix flickering redraws * fix(SyncProcess): Fix reorder reconciliation * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Abort read requests on cancel * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix handling of javascript bookmarks * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix update{Bookmark,Folder} to not reorder without parent changes * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix sendOCSRequest * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix removeBookmark * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): correct createBookmark bookkeeping * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix locking and cancel logic * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Fix hash function handling * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Don't fail if parent in update{Folder,Bookmark} can't be found * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Don't fail if parent in updateBookmark can't be found * fix(SyncProcess): Make sure we map the contents of a folder re-created because its origin was removed * fix(Linkwarden): Do not leak isRoot flags for non-root folders * fix(NextcloudBookmarks#bulkImport): Make sure all chunked bulkImports get added to the target folder, not just the last * fix(bulkImport): Make sure chunked bulkImports get added to mappings * fix(Continuation): Don't apply failsafe for resumed sync runs * fix(Continuation): Don't fail sync if continuation can't be read * fix(Continuation): Check the right strategy when resuming * fix(Continuation): Resume at reorder stage if reorders are persisted * fix(Continuation): Persist continuation one last time upon sync failure * fix(NativeCacheStore): Don't drop statements when saving fails * fix(SyncProcess): Correct stage 3 members to persist condition * fix(Diff): Copy actions and seqs in getPendingChangesAsync * fix(tests): Make order check adapter dependent * fix(SyncProcess): Make failsafe less agressive for tab sync * fix(Scanner): Allow moving a folder into its own descendant iff it was moved out of the folder * fix(NextcloudBookmarks): Don't stop loading sparse tree too early * fix: enhance snapshot management in Mappings * fix: Introduce clearContinuation, resolve quadratic tree scans at sync start * fix: Throw LocalFolderNotFound instead of creating random folders, do not disable newly-imported profiles * fix(BrowserTree): Do not move bookmarks to end of folder upon rename * fix(Dropbox): Retry 500 errors * fix: Cleanup mappings for missing local and server items * fix: Fix race in SQLite/IndexedDB continuation code * fix(Tree#updateIndex): Rebuild item index to account for adapter ID rewriting * fix(Linkwarden): Retry requests for 5xx responses 
```

### 2026-09-28T01:32:59Z [**zmister2016/MrDoc**](https://github.com/zmister2016/MrDoc)

```
* [Fix] Fixed a potential remote command execution vulnerability caused by insufficient content escaping during PDF export; * [Fix] Fixed an issue where the User Token API for retrieving document lists only returned documents created by the current user; * [Fix] Fixed a file reading vulnerability during Word import that could occur when using older versions of `mammonth`; * [Feat] Fixed an issue where document collection export did not follow the corresponding backend configuration switch; * [Feat] Optimized the timing of image loading and rendering during PDF export of document collections; * [Feat] Optimized the RAG retrieval debugger by adding detailed results for each retrieval branch and retrieval scope filtering; * [Feat] Document collections now support unlimited nesting levels; * [Feat] Optimized the default uWSGI configuration; * [Feat] Rebuilt the document collection directory in the document editor using jsTree, adding support for drag-and-drop sorting, context menus, and other operations; * [Feat] Updated the required Docker image and dependency versions. 
```
